Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a *New Phyrexia / Mirrodin Besieged / Scars of Mirrodin* draft from the Top 8 of a Premier Event on [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20140527070414/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da763_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110727000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=2608932)
